

export_script_for_pool = {
	clear_array = oil_produced_prior
	clear_array = oil_export_prior
	clear_array = food_produced_prior
	clear_array = food_export_prior
	clear_array = steel_produced_prior
	clear_array = steel_export_prior
	clear_array = chromium_produced_prior
	clear_array = chromium_export_prior
	clear_array = aluminium_produced_prior
	clear_array = aluminium_export_prior
	clear_array = rareEarth_produced_prior
	clear_array = rareEarth_export_prior

	every_other_country = {	
		set_variable = { chromiumExportRa1e = modifier@chromium_export_rate }
		set_variable = { oilExportRate = modifier@oil_export_rate }
		set_variable = { foodExportRate = modifier@food_export_rate }
		set_variable = { steelExportRate = modifier@steel_export_rate }
		set_variable = { aluminiumExportRate = modifier@aluminum_export_rate }
		set_variable = { rareEarthExportRate = modifier@rare_earth_export_rate }
		set_variable = { consumerGoodsExportRate = modifier@consumer_goods_export_rate }

		set_variable = { oilExport = 0 }
		set_variable = { foodExport = 0 }
		set_variable = { steelExport = 0 }
		set_variable = { chromiumExport = 0 }
		set_variable = { aluminiumExport = 0 }
		set_variable = { rareEarthExport = 0 }

		# ------------------------------------------------------------
		# Single controlled state resource pass
		# ------------------------------------------------------------
		every_controlled_state = {
			add_to_variable = { PREV.oilExport = resource@oil }
			add_to_variable = { PREV.foodExport = resource@rubber }
			add_to_variable = { PREV.steelExport = resource@steel }
			add_to_variable = { PREV.chromiumExport = resource@chromium }
			add_to_variable = { PREV.aluminiumExport = resource@aluminium }
			add_to_variable = { PREV.rareEarthExport = resource@tungsten }
		}

		# ------------------------------------------------------------
		# Oil Section
		# ------------------------------------------------------------
		subtract_from_variable = { oilExport = oil_consumption }

		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { oilExport < 2 }
			}
			set_variable = { oilExport = 0 }
		}

		round_variable = oilExport

		multiply_variable = { oilExport = oilExportRate }
		round_variable = oilExport

		# ------------------------------------------------------------
		# Food Section
		# ------------------------------------------------------------
		round_variable = foodExport

		multiply_variable = { foodExport = foodExportRate }

		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { foodExport < 2 }
			}
			set_variable = { foodExport = 0 }
		}

		round_variable = foodExport

		# ------------------------------------------------------------
		# Steel Section
		# ------------------------------------------------------------
		subtract_from_variable = { steelExport = steel_consumption }

		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { steelExport < 2 }
			}
			set_variable = { steelExport = 0 }
		}

		round_variable = steelExport

		multiply_variable = { steelExport = steelExportRate }
		round_variable = steelExport

		# ------------------------------------------------------------
		# Nuclear / Chromium Section
		# ------------------------------------------------------------
		subtract_from_variable = { chromiumExport = chromium_consumption }

		round_variable = chromiumExport

		multiply_variable = { chromiumExport = chromiumExportRa1e }

		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { chromiumExport < 2 }
			}
			set_variable = { chromiumExport = 0 }
		}

		round_variable = chromiumExport

		# ------------------------------------------------------------
		# Aluminium Section
		# ------------------------------------------------------------
		set_variable = { aluminium_test = aluminiumExport }

		subtract_from_variable = { aluminiumExport = aluminium_consumption }

		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { aluminiumExport < 2 }
			}
			set_variable = { aluminiumExport = 0 }
		}

		round_variable = aluminiumExport

		multiply_variable = { aluminiumExport = aluminiumExportRate }
		round_variable = aluminiumExport

		# ------------------------------------------------------------
		# Rare Earth Section
		# ------------------------------------------------------------
		subtract_from_variable = { rareEarthExport = rare_earth_consumption }

		round_variable = rareEarthExport

		multiply_variable = { rareEarthExport = rareEarthExportRate }

		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { rareEarthExport < 2 }
			}
			set_variable = { rareEarthExport = 0 }
		}

		round_variable = rareEarthExport

		# ------------------------------------------------------------
		# Current Export Rate Calcs
		# ------------------------------------------------------------
		set_variable = { currentAvailableoilExport = oilExport }
		round_variable = currentAvailableoilExport
		subtract_from_variable = { currentAvailableoilExport = currentExportedoil }
		country_event = trade.2
		
		set_variable = { currentAvailableFoodExport = foodExport }
		round_variable = currentAvailableFoodExport
		subtract_from_variable = { currentAvailableFoodExport = currentExportedFood }

		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { currentAvailableFoodExport < 0 }
			}
			country_event = trade.7
		}

		set_variable = { currentAvailableSteelExport = steelExport }
		round_variable = currentAvailableSteelExport
		subtract_from_variable = { currentAvailableSteelExport = currentExportedSteel }

		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { currentAvailableSteelExport < 0 }
			}
			country_event = trade.3 
		}

		set_variable = { currentAvailableChromiumExport = chromiumExport }
		round_variable = currentAvailableChromiumExport
		subtract_from_variable = { currentAvailableChromiumExport = currentExportedChromium }

		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { currentAvailableChromiumExport < 0 }
			}
			country_event = trade.6
		}

		set_variable = { currentAvailableAluminiumExport = aluminiumExport }
		round_variable = currentAvailableAluminiumExport
		subtract_from_variable = { currentAvailableAluminiumExport = currentExportedAluminium }

		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { currentAvailableAluminiumExport < 0 }
			}
			country_event = trade.5
		}

		set_variable = { currentAvailableRareEarthExport = rareEarthExport }
		round_variable = currentAvailableRareEarthExport
		subtract_from_variable = { currentAvailableRareEarthExport = currentExportedRareEarth }

		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { currentAvailableRareEarthExport < 0 }
			}
			country_event = trade.4 
		}
	}
}

startup_price_setting = { 
	set_variable = { global.oilBasePrice = 0.2 }
	set_variable = { global.steelBasePrice = 0.1 }
	set_variable = { global.foodBasePrice = 0.15 }
	set_variable = { global.chromiumBasePrice = 2 }
	set_variable = { global.rareEarthBasePrice = 0.3 }
	set_variable = { global.aluminiumBasePrice = 0.1 }

	# Consumer Goods Prices
	set_variable = { global.cgBasicT0BasePrice = 0.03 }
	set_variable = { global.cgDurableT0BasePrice = 0.08 }
	set_variable = { global.cgVehiclesT0BasePrice = 0.35 }

	set_variable = { global.cgBasicT1BasePrice = 0.05 }
	set_variable = { global.cgBasicT2BasePrice = 0.08 }
	set_variable = { global.cgBasicT3BasePrice = 0.12 }
	set_variable = { global.cgBasicT4BasePrice = 0.18 }
	set_variable = { global.cgBasicT5BasePrice = 0.25 }

	set_variable = { global.cgDurableT1BasePrice = 0.12 }
	set_variable = { global.cgDurableT2BasePrice = 0.20 }
	set_variable = { global.cgDurableT3BasePrice = 0.35 }
	set_variable = { global.cgDurableT4BasePrice = 0.55 }
	set_variable = { global.cgDurableT5BasePrice = 0.80 }

	set_variable = { global.cgVehiclesT1BasePrice = 0.50 }
	set_variable = { global.cgVehiclesT2BasePrice = 0.85 }
	set_variable = { global.cgVehiclesT3BasePrice = 1.25 }
	set_variable = { global.cgVehiclesT4BasePrice = 1.75 }
	set_variable = { global.cgVehiclesT5BasePrice = 2.50 }

	set_variable = { global.cgElectronicsT1BasePrice = 0.20 }
	set_variable = { global.cgElectronicsT2BasePrice = 0.35 }
	set_variable = { global.cgElectronicsT3BasePrice = 0.60 }
	set_variable = { global.cgElectronicsT4BasePrice = 0.95 }
	set_variable = { global.cgElectronicsT5BasePrice = 1.40 }
}
test_script = {
	startup_price_setting = yes
	USA = {
		export_script_for_pool = yes
		buy_nuclear_material = yes
	}
}
generate_available_chromium_import_array = {
	clear_array = ROOT.chromiumExporters
	clear_array = ROOT.chromiumExporterInfo
	every_other_country = {
		limit = {
            OR = {
                check_variable = {
                    currentAvailableChromiumExport > 0.99
                }
                check_variable = {
                    currentExportedChromium > 0.99
                }
            }

			NOT = {
				tag = ROOT
			}
			check_variable = {
				ROOT.trade_embargo_check^THIS = 0
			}
			NOT = {
				has_war_with = ROOT
			}
		}
		clear_array = chromiumCurrentExport
		for_loop_effect = {
			end = chromiumExportTarget^num
			value = v
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { chromiumExportTarget^v = ROOT.id }
				}
				add_to_array = { chromiumCurrentExport = chromiumExportTargetAmount^i }

			}
			else = {
				add_to_array = { chromiumCurrentExport = 0 }

			}
		}
		
		add_to_array = {
			ROOT.chromiumExporters = THIS.id
		}
		add_to_array = {
			ROOT.chromiumExporterInfo = THIS.currentAvailableChromiumExport
		}
		set_variable = {
			chromiumExportCash = global.chromiumBasePrice
		}
		set_variable = {
			price_Adj = 0
		}
		set_temp_variable = {
			price_Adjuster = 0
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			price_Adjuster = ROOT.price_adjust_chromium^THIS
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_subject_of = ROOT
			}
			set_variable = {
				price_Adj = modifier@extra_trade_to_overlord_factor
			}
			set_variable = {
				price_Adj2 = 1
			}
			subtract_from_variable = {
				price_Adj2 = price_Adj
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				price_Adjuster = 1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					price_Adjuster = 1
				}
			}
			multiply_variable = {
				chromiumExportCash = price_Adj
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					check_variable = {
						ROOT.price_adjust_generic^THIS = 0
					}
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				price_Adjuster = 0
			}
			set_variable = {
				price_Adjuster_Special = ROOT.price_adjust_generic^THIS
			}
			set_variable = {
				price_Adj = price_Adjuster_Special
			}
			set_variable = {
				price_Adj2 = 1
			}
			subtract_from_variable = {
				price_Adj2 = price_Adj
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				price_Adjuster = 1
			}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					price_Adjuster = 1
				}
			}
			multiply_variable = {
				chromiumExportCash = price_Adj
			}
		}

		set_variable = {
			price_adjustment_ideas = modifier@global_trade_penalty_adjustment
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = arab_league_member
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				price_adjustment_ideas = ROOT.modifier@arab_league_trade_penalty_adjustment
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			price_adjustment_ideas = 1
		}
		multiply_variable = {
			chromiumExportCash = price_adjustment_ideas
		}
		set_variable = {
			price_adjustment_ideas2 = 1
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			price_adjustment_ideas2 = modifier@global_trade_bonus_adjustment
		}
		multiply_variable = {
			chromiumExportCash = price_adjustment_ideas2
		}
        #Shipping Cost
        set_temp_variable = { exporter_capital = capital }
        set_variable = { exp_test = capital }
        var:exporter_capital = {
            set_variable = { PREV.shipping_cost = distance_to@ROOT.capital } #This needs to be a normal variable
        }
        
        divide_variable = { shipping_cost = global.base_shipping_cost_divisor }
        multiply_variable = {
            shipping_cost = 100
        }
        round_variable = shipping_cost
        divide_variable = {
            shipping_cost = 100
        }
        set_temp_variable = { modifier_temp = shipping_cost_effect }
        add_to_temp_variable = { modifier_temp = 1 }
        multiply_variable = { shipping_cost = modifier_temp }
        add_to_variable = { shipping_cost = 1 }

        set_temp_variable = { true_price = chromiumExportCash }
        multiply_temp_variable = { true_price = shipping_cost }

        add_to_variable = {
            chromiumExportCash = true_price
        }


		clear_variable = price_Adjuster
		set_variable = {
			chromiumExporterPrice^ROOT = chromiumExportCash
		}
	}
}

buy_nuclear_material = {

	var:currentPurchaser = {
		for_loop_effect = {
			end = ROOT.Nuclear_Import_List
			value = v
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { Nuclear_Import_List^v = THIS.id }
				}
				set_country_flag = found
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = found } }
			add_to_array = { ROOT.Nuclear_Import_List = THIS.id }
		}
		clr_country_flag = found
		for_loop_effect = {
			end = Nuclear_Export_List
			value = v
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { Nuclear_Export_List^v = ROOT.id }
				}
				set_country_flag = found
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = found } }
			add_to_array = { Nuclear_Export_List = ROOT.id }
		}
		clr_country_flag = found
		subtract_from_variable = {
			ROOT.purchase_amount = ROOT.purchase_amount_prior
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			currentAvailableChromiumExport = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			ROOT.trade_infrastructure_total_used = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			ROOT.trade_infrastructure_total_available = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			currentExportedChromium = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			ROOT.nuclearImports = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		set_variable = {
			chromiumExportTarget^ROOT = ROOT.id
		} 
		add_to_variable = {
			chromiumExportTargetAmount^ROOT = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		set_variable = {
			price_of_goods = chromiumExporterPrice^ROOT
		}
		multiply_variable = {
			price_of_goods = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			chromiumExportTargetMonthly^ROOT = price_of_goods
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			ROOT.chromiumImportTargetMonthly^THIS = price_of_goods
		}
		set_variable = {
			ROOT.chromiumImportTarget^THIS = THIS.id
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			ROOT.chromiumImportTargetAmount^THIS = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		set_variable = {
			price_of_goods = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			ROOT.purchase_amount = 0
		}
	}
	every_other_country = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				currentAvailableChromiumExport > 0.99
			}
			NOT = {
				tag = ROOT
			}
			check_variable = {
				ROOT.trade_embargo_check^THIS = 0
			}
			NOT = {
				has_war_with = ROOT
			}
		}
		clear_array = chromiumCurrentExport
		for_loop_effect = {
			end = chromiumExportTarget^num
			value = v
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { chromiumExportTarget^v = ROOT.id }
				}
				add_to_array = { chromiumCurrentExport = chromiumExportTargetAmount^i }

			}
			else = {
				add_to_array = { chromiumCurrentExport = 0 }

			}
		}
	}
	
}
sort_price_number_aluminium = {
    #clear_array = aluminiumCurrentImport
    clear_array = aluminiumExportPriceRate
    
    for_each_scope_loop = {
        array = aluminiumExporters
        #end = aluminiumExporters^num
        #value = v
        add_to_array = { 
            PREV.aluminiumExportPriceRate = aluminiumExporterPrice^ROOT
        }
    }
	clear_array = aluminiumCurrentImport
	every_other_country = { 
		for_loop_effect = {
			end = aluminiumExportTarget^num
			value = v
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { aluminiumExportTarget^v = ROOT.id }
				}
				add_to_array = { ROOT.aluminiumCurrentImport = aluminiumExportTargetAmount^i }

			}
			else = {
				add_to_array = { ROOT.aluminiumCurrentImport = 0 }
			}
		}
	}
	#Creates the Sample Array	
	resize_temp_array = {
		array = aluminiumExporterInfo_temp #values
		size = aluminiumExporterInfo^num
	}
	resize_temp_array = {
		array = aluminiumExportPriceRate_temp #values 2
		size = aluminiumExporterInfo^num
	}
	resize_temp_array = {
		array = aluminiumExporters_temp #tags
		size = aluminiumExporterInfo^num
	}

    set_temp_variable = { start_temp = aluminiumExportPriceRate^num }
    subtract_from_temp_variable = { start_temp = 1 }
    for_loop_effect = {
        start = start_temp
        end = 0
        compare = greater_than_or_equals
        add = -1
        value = v
		find_highest_in_array = {
			array = aluminiumExportPriceRate
			value = max
			index = max_index
		}
		set_temp_variable = { aluminiumExportPriceRate_temp^v = max } #sets biggest at the earliest available position
		set_temp_variable = { aluminiumExporterInfo_temp^v = aluminiumExporters^max_index } #sets biggest TAG at the earliest available position
		set_temp_variable = { aluminiumExporters_temp^v = aluminiumExporters^max_index } #sets biggest TAG at the earliest available position
		set_variable = { aluminiumExportPriceRate^max_index = -1 } #removes it from selection
	}
	clear_array = aluminiumExporterInfo
	clear_array = aluminiumExporters
    clear_array = aluminiumExportPriceRate
	#restores destroyed arrays in the above process - i.e. if they were deleted due to being less then 1%
	for_each_loop = {
		array = aluminiumExportPriceRate_temp
		add_to_array = { aluminiumExportPriceRate = v }
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = aluminiumExporterInfo_temp
		add_to_array = { aluminiumExporterInfo = v }
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = aluminiumExporters_temp
		add_to_array = { aluminiumExporters = v  }
	}
	clear_temp_array = aluminiumExporterInfo_temp
	clear_temp_array = aluminiumExporters_temp
    clear_temp_array = aluminiumExportPriceRate_temp
}

    #clear_array = chromiumCurrentImport
    clear_array = chromiumExportPriceRate
    
    for_each_scope_loop = {
        array = chromiumExporters
        #end = chromiumExporters^num
        #value = v
        add_to_array = {

        }
    }
	clear_array = chromiumCurrentImport
	every_other_country = {

			end = chromiumExportTarget^num
			value = v
			if = {

		array = chromiumExportPriceRate_temp #values 2
		size = chromiumExporterInfo^num

	resize_temp_array = {
		array = chromiumExporters_temp #tags
		size = chromiumExporterInfo^num

    set_temp_variable = { start_temp = chromiumExportPriceRate^num }

    for_loop_effect = {
        start = start_temp
        end = 0
        compare = greater_than_or_equals
        add = -1
        value = v
		find_highest_in_array = {
			array = chromiumExportPriceRate
			value = max
			index = max_index
		}
		set_temp_variable = { chromiumExportPriceRate_temp^v = max } #sets biggest at the earliest available position
		set_temp_variable = { chromiumExporterInfo_temp^v = chromiumExporters^max_index } #sets biggest TAG at the earliest available position
