buy_aluminium = {

	var:currentPurchaser = {
		for_loop_effect = {
			end = ROOT.Aluminium_Import_List
			value = v
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { Aluminium_Import_List^v = THIS.id }
				}
				set_country_flag = found
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = found } }
			add_to_array = { ROOT.Aluminium_Import_List = THIS.id }
		}
		clr_country_flag = found
		for_loop_effect = {
			end = Aluminium_Export_List
			value = v
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { Aluminium_Export_List^v = ROOT.id }
				}
				set_country_flag = found
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = found } }
			add_to_array = { Aluminium_Export_List = ROOT.id }
		}
		clr_country_flag = found

		subtract_from_variable = {
			ROOT.purchase_amount = ROOT.purchase_amount_prior
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			currentAvailablealuminiumExport = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			ROOT.trade_infrastructure_total_used = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			ROOT.trade_infrastructure_total_available = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			currentExportedaluminium = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			ROOT.aluminiumImports = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		set_variable = {
			aluminiumExportTarget^ROOT = ROOT.id
		} 
		add_to_variable = {
			aluminiumExportTargetAmount^ROOT = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		set_variable = {
			price_of_goods = aluminiumExporterPrice^ROOT
		}
		multiply_variable = {
			price_of_goods = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			aluminiumExportTargetMonthly^ROOT = price_of_goods
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			ROOT.aluminiumImportTargetMonthly^THIS = price_of_goods
		}
		set_variable = {
			ROOT.aluminiumImportTarget^THIS = THIS.id
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			ROOT.aluminiumImportTargetAmount^THIS = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		set_variable = {
			price_of_goods = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			ROOT.purchase_amount = 0
		}
	}
	every_other_country = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				currentAvailablealuminiumExport > 0.99
			}
			NOT = {
				tag = ROOT
			}
			check_variable = {
				ROOT.trade_embargo_check^THIS = 0
			}
			NOT = {
				has_war_with = ROOT
			}
		}
		clear_array = aluminiumCurrentExport
		for_loop_effect = {
			end = aluminiumExportTarget^num
			value = v
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { aluminiumExportTarget^v = ROOT.id }
				}
				add_to_array = { aluminiumCurrentExport = aluminiumExportTargetAmount^i }

			}
			else = {
				add_to_array = { aluminiumCurrentExport = 0 }

			}
		}
	}
	
}
##Rare Earth##


generate_available_rareEarth_import_array = {
	clear_array = ROOT.rareEarthExporters
	clear_array = ROOT.rareEarthExporterInfo
	every_other_country = {
		limit = {
            OR = {
                check_variable = {
                    currentAvailablerareEarthExport > 0.99
                }
                check_variable = {
                    currentExportedrareEarth > 0.99
                }
            }
			NOT = {
				tag = ROOT
			}
			check_variable = {
				ROOT.trade_embargo_check^THIS = 0
			}
			NOT = {
				has_war_with = ROOT
			}
		}
		clear_array = rareEarthCurrentExport
		for_loop_effect = {
			end = rareEarthExportTarget^num
			value = v
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { rareEarthExportTarget^v = ROOT.id }
				}
				add_to_array = { rareEarthCurrentExport = rareEarthExportTargetAmount^i }

			}
			else = {
				add_to_array = { rareEarthCurrentExport = 0 }

			}
		}
		
		add_to_array = {
			ROOT.rareEarthExporters = THIS.id
		}
		add_to_array = {
			ROOT.rareEarthExporterInfo = THIS.currentAvailablerareEarthExport
		}
		set_variable = {
			rareEarthExportCash = global.rareEarthBasePrice
		}
		set_variable = {
			price_Adj = 0
		}
		set_temp_variable = {
			price_Adjuster = 0
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			price_Adjuster = ROOT.price_adjust_rare_earth^THIS
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_subject_of = ROOT
			}
			set_variable = {
				price_Adj = modifier@extra_trade_to_overlord_factor
			}
			set_variable = {
				price_Adj2 = 1
			}
			subtract_from_variable = {
				price_Adj2 = price_Adj
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				price_Adjuster = 1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					price_Adjuster = 1
				}
			}
			multiply_variable = {
				rareEarthExportCash = price_Adj
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					check_variable = {
						ROOT.price_adjust_generic^THIS = 0
					}
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				price_Adjuster = 0
			}
			set_variable = {
				price_Adjuster_Special = ROOT.price_adjust_generic^THIS
			}
			set_variable = {
				price_Adj = price_Adjuster_Special
			}
			set_variable = {
				price_Adj2 = 1
			}
			subtract_from_variable = {
				price_Adj2 = price_Adj
			}
			set_temp_variable = {
				price_Adjuster = 1
			}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					price_Adjuster = 1
				}
			}
			multiply_variable = {
				rareEarthExportCash = price_Adj
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			price_adjustment_ideas = modifier@global_trade_penalty_adjustment
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = arab_league_member
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				price_adjustment_ideas = ROOT.modifier@arab_league_trade_penalty_adjustment
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			price_adjustment_ideas = 1
		}
		multiply_variable = {
			rareEarthExportCash = price_adjustment_ideas
		}
		set_variable = {
			price_adjustment_ideas2 = 1
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			price_adjustment_ideas2 = modifier@global_trade_bonus_adjustment
		}
		multiply_variable = {
			rareEarthExportCash = price_adjustment_ideas2
		}
        #Shipping Cost
        set_temp_variable = { exporter_capital = capital }
        set_variable = { exp_test = capital }
        var:exporter_capital = {
            set_variable = { PREV.shipping_cost = distance_to@ROOT.capital } #This needs to be a normal variable
        }
        
        divide_variable = { shipping_cost = global.base_shipping_cost_divisor }
        multiply_variable = {
            shipping_cost = 100
        }
        round_variable = shipping_cost
        divide_variable = {
            shipping_cost = 100
        }
        set_temp_variable = { modifier_temp = shipping_cost_effect }
        add_to_temp_variable = { modifier_temp = 1 }
        multiply_variable = { shipping_cost = modifier_temp }
        add_to_variable = { shipping_cost = 1 }

        set_temp_variable = { true_price = rareEarthExportCash }
        multiply_temp_variable = { true_price = shipping_cost }

        add_to_variable = {
            rareEarthExportCash = true_price
        }


		clear_variable = price_Adjuster
		set_variable = {
			rareEarthExporterPrice^ROOT = rareEarthExportCash
		}
	}
}

buy_rareEarth = {

	var:currentPurchaser = {
		for_loop_effect = {
			end = ROOT.RareEarth_Import_List
			value = v
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { RareEarth_Import_List^v = THIS.id }
				}
				set_country_flag = found
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = found } }
			add_to_array = { ROOT.RareEarth_Import_List = THIS.id }
		}
		clr_country_flag = found
		for_loop_effect = {
			end = RareEarth_Export_List
			value = v
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { RareEarth_Export_List^v = ROOT.id }
				}
				set_country_flag = found
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = found } }
			add_to_array = { RareEarth_Export_List = ROOT.id }
		}
		clr_country_flag = found

		subtract_from_variable = {
			ROOT.purchase_amount = ROOT.purchase_amount_prior
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			currentAvailablerareEarthExport = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			ROOT.trade_infrastructure_total_used = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			ROOT.trade_infrastructure_total_available = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			currentExportedrareEarth = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			ROOT.rareEarthImports = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		set_variable = {
			rareEarthExportTarget^ROOT = ROOT.id
		} 
		add_to_variable = {
			rareEarthExportTargetAmount^ROOT = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		set_variable = {
			price_of_goods = rareEarthExporterPrice^ROOT
		}
		multiply_variable = {
			price_of_goods = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			rareEarthExportTargetMonthly^ROOT = price_of_goods
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			ROOT.rareEarthImportTargetMonthly^THIS = price_of_goods
		}
		set_variable = {
			ROOT.rareEarthImportTarget^THIS = THIS.id
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			ROOT.rareEarthImportTargetAmount^THIS = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		set_variable = {
			price_of_goods = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			ROOT.purchase_amount = 0
		}
	}
	every_other_country = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				currentAvailablerareEarthExport > 0.99
			}
			NOT = {
				tag = ROOT
			}
			check_variable = {
				ROOT.trade_embargo_check^THIS = 0
			}
			NOT = {
				has_war_with = ROOT
			}
		}
		clear_array = rareEarthCurrentExport
		for_loop_effect = {
			end = rareEarthExportTarget^num
			value = v
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { rareEarthExportTarget^v = ROOT.id }
				}
				add_to_array = { rareEarthCurrentExport = rareEarthExportTargetAmount^i }

			}
			else = {
				add_to_array = { rareEarthCurrentExport = 0 }

			}
		}
	}
	
}


#rareEarthExporterPrice
sort_price_number_rareEarth = {
    #clear_array = rareEarthCurrentImport
    clear_array = rareEarthExportPriceRate
    
    for_each_scope_loop = {
        array = rareEarthExporters
        #end = rareEarthExporters^num
        #value = v
        add_to_array = { 
            PREV.rareEarthExportPriceRate = rareEarthExporterPrice^ROOT
        }
    }
	clear_array = rareEarthCurrentImport
	every_other_country = { 
		for_loop_effect = {
			end = rareEarthExportTarget^num
			value = v
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { rareEarthExportTarget^v = ROOT.id }
				}
				add_to_array = { ROOT.rareEarthCurrentImport = rareEarthExportTargetAmount^i }

			}
			else = {
				add_to_array = { ROOT.rareEarthCurrentImport = 0 }
			}
		}
	}
	#Creates the Sample Array	
	resize_temp_array = {
		array = rareEarthExporterInfo_temp #values
		size = rareEarthExporterInfo^num
	}
	resize_temp_array = {
		array = rareEarthExportPriceRate_temp #values 2
		size = rareEarthExporterInfo^num
	}
	resize_temp_array = {
		array = rareEarthExporters_temp #tags
		size = rareEarthExporterInfo^num
	}

    set_temp_variable = { start_temp = rareEarthExportPriceRate^num }
    subtract_from_temp_variable = { start_temp = 1 }
    for_loop_effect = {
        start = start_temp
        end = 0
        compare = greater_than_or_equals
        add = -1
        value = v
		find_highest_in_array = {
			array = rareEarthExportPriceRate
			value = max
			index = max_index
		}
		set_temp_variable = { rareEarthExportPriceRate_temp^v = max } #sets biggest at the earliest available position
		set_temp_variable = { rareEarthExporterInfo_temp^v = rareEarthExporters^max_index } #sets biggest TAG at the earliest available position
		set_temp_variable = { rareEarthExporters_temp^v = rareEarthExporters^max_index } #sets biggest TAG at the earliest available position
		set_variable = { rareEarthExportPriceRate^max_index = -1 } #removes it from selection
	}
	clear_array = rareEarthExporterInfo
	clear_array = rareEarthExporters
    clear_array = rareEarthExportPriceRate
	#restores destroyed arrays in the above process - i.e. if they were deleted due to being less then 1%
	for_each_loop = {
		array = rareEarthExportPriceRate_temp
		add_to_array = { rareEarthExportPriceRate = v }
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = rareEarthExporterInfo_temp
		add_to_array = { rareEarthExporterInfo = v }
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = rareEarthExporters_temp
		add_to_array = { rareEarthExporters = v  }
	}
	clear_temp_array = rareEarthExporterInfo_temp
	clear_temp_array = rareEarthExporters_temp
    clear_temp_array = rareEarthExportPriceRate_temp
}

sort_resource_number_rareEarth = {
	clear_array = rareEarthCurrentImport
	every_other_country = { 
		for_loop_effect = {
			end = rareEarthExportTarget^num
			value = v
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { rareEarthExportTarget^v = ROOT.id }
				}
				add_to_array = { ROOT.rareEarthCurrentImport = rareEarthExportTargetAmount^i }

			}
			else = {
				add_to_array = { ROOT.rareEarthCurrentImport = 0 }
			}
		}
	}
	#Creates the Sample Array	
	resize_temp_array = {
		array = rareEarthExporterInfo_temp #values
		size = rareEarthExporterInfo^num
	}
	resize_temp_array = {
		array = rareEarthExporters_temp #tags
		size = rareEarthExporterInfo^num
	}

	for_loop_effect = {
		end = rareEarthExporterInfo^num
		value = v
		find_highest_in_array = {
			array = rareEarthExporterInfo
			value = max
			index = max_index
		}
		set_temp_variable = { rareEarthExporterInfo_temp^v = max } #sets biggest at the earliest available position
		set_temp_variable = { rareEarthExporters_temp^v = rareEarthExporters^max_index } #sets biggest TAG at the earliest available position
		set_variable = { rareEarthExporterInfo^max_index = -1 } #removes it from selection
	}
	clear_array = rareEarthExporterInfo
	clear_array = rareEarthExporters
	#restores destroyed arrays in the above process - i.e. if they were deleted due to being less then 1%
	for_each_loop = {
		array = rareEarthExporterInfo_temp
		add_to_array = { rareEarthExporterInfo = v }
		
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = rareEarthExporters_temp
		add_to_array = { rareEarthExporters = v  }
	}
	clear_temp_array = rareEarthExporterInfo_temp
	clear_temp_array = rareEarthExporters_temp



}


rareEarth_import_ai = {
	set_variable = {
		rareEarth_total = resource@tungsten
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				rareEarth_total < 0
			}
			check_variable = {
				trade_infrastructure_total_available > 0
			}
		}
		generate_available_rareEarth_import_array = yes
        sort_price_number_rareEarth = yes

        #Loops thru cheapest countries to buy from
        for_each_scope_loop = {
            array = ROOT.rareEarthExporters
            #index = i
            break = cheapest_brk

            #Checks if the country's goods are cheaper & shipping is affordabole 
            #TODO - Shipping cost
            if = {
                limit = {
                    check_variable = { currentAvailablerareEarthExport > 0 }
                    OR = {
                        NOT = { check_variable = { ROOT.trade_infrastructure_total_available < 1 } }
                        NOT = { check_variable = { ROOT.rareEarth_total > -1 } }
                    }
                }
                set_variable = {
                    rareEarth_need = ROOT.rareEarth_total
                }
				multiply_variable = {
					rareEarth_need = -1
				}
                if = {
                    limit = {
                        check_variable = {
                            currentAvailablerareEarthExport > rareEarth_need
                        }
                    }

                    set_variable = {
                        ROOT.purchase_amount = rareEarth_need
                    }
                    add_to_variable = {
                        ROOT.rareEarth_total = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }

                    subtract_from_variable = {
                        ROOT.purchase_amount = ROOT.purchase_amount_prior
                    }
                    subtract_from_variable = {
                        currentAvailablerareEarthExport = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }
                    add_to_variable = {
                        ROOT.trade_infrastructure_total_used = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }
                    subtract_from_variable = {
                        ROOT.trade_infrastructure_total_available = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }
                    add_to_variable = {
                        currentExportedrareEarth = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }
                    add_to_variable = {
                        ROOT.rareEarthImports = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        rareEarthExportTarget^ROOT = ROOT.id
                    } 
                    add_to_variable = {
                        rareEarthExportTargetAmount^ROOT = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        price_of_goods = rareEarthExporterPrice^ROOT
                    }
                    multiply_variable = {
                        price_of_goods = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }
                    add_to_variable = {
                        rareEarthExportTargetMonthly^ROOT = price_of_goods
                    }
                    add_to_variable = {
                        ROOT.rareEarthImportTargetMonthly^THIS = price_of_goods
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        ROOT.rareEarthImportTarget^THIS = THIS.id
                    }
                    add_to_variable = {
                        ROOT.rareEarthImportTargetAmount^THIS = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        price_of_goods = 0
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        ROOT.purchase_amount = 0
                    }
					for_loop_effect = {
						end = ROOT.RareEarth_Import_List
						value = v
						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { RareEarth_Import_List^v = THIS.id }
							}
							set_country_flag = found
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = found } }
						add_to_array = { ROOT.RareEarth_Import_List = THIS.id }
					}
					clr_country_flag = found
					for_loop_effect = {
						end = RareEarth_Export_List
						value = v
						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { RareEarth_Export_List^v = ROOT.id }
							}
							set_country_flag = found
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = found } }
						add_to_array = { RareEarth_Export_List = ROOT.id }
					}
					clr_country_flag = found
					if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = {
								currentAvailablerareEarthExport < 0
							}
						}
						country_event = trade.4
					}
                }
                else = {
                    set_variable = {
                        rareEarth_need = currentAvailablerareEarthExport
                    }			
                    set_variable = {
                        ROOT.purchase_amount = rareEarth_need
                    }
                    add_to_variable = {
                        ROOT.rareEarth_total = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }
                    subtract_from_variable = {
                        ROOT.purchase_amount = ROOT.purchase_amount_prior
                    }
                    subtract_from_variable = {
                        currentAvailablerareEarthExport = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }
                    add_to_variable = {
                        ROOT.trade_infrastructure_total_used = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }
                    subtract_from_variable = {
                        ROOT.trade_infrastructure_total_available = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }
                    add_to_variable = {
                        currentExportedrareEarth = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }
                    add_to_variable = {
                        ROOT.rareEarthImports = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        rareEarthExportTarget^ROOT = ROOT.id
                    } 
                    add_to_variable = {
                        rareEarthExportTargetAmount^ROOT = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        price_of_goods = rareEarthExporterPrice^ROOT
                    }
                    multiply_variable = {
                        price_of_goods = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }
                    add_to_variable = {
                        rareEarthExportTargetMonthly^ROOT = price_of_goods
                    }
                    add_to_variable = {
                        ROOT.rareEarthImportTargetMonthly^THIS = price_of_goods
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        ROOT.rareEarthImportTarget^THIS = THIS.id
                    }
                    add_to_variable = {
                        ROOT.rareEarthImportTargetAmount^THIS = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        price_of_goods = 0
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        ROOT.purchase_amount = 0
                    }
					for_loop_effect = {
						end = ROOT.RareEarth_Import_List
						value = v
						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { RareEarth_Import_List^v = THIS.id }
							}
							set_country_flag = found
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = found } }
						add_to_array = { ROOT.RareEarth_Import_List = THIS.id }
					}
					clr_country_flag = found
					for_loop_effect = {
						end = RareEarth_Export_List
						value = v
						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { RareEarth_Export_List^v = ROOT.id }
							}
							set_country_flag = found
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = found } }
						add_to_array = { RareEarth_Export_List = ROOT.id }
					}
					clr_country_flag = found
					if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = {
								currentAvailablerareEarthExport < 0
							}
						}
						country_event = trade.4
					}
                }
            }
           if = {
                limit = {
                    OR = {
                        NOT = { check_variable = { ROOT.trade_infrastructure_total_available < 1 } }
                        NOT = { check_variable = { ROOT.rareEarth_total > -1 } }
                    }
                }
                set_temp_variable = { cheapest_brk = 1 }
            }
        }
	}
}
#aluminiumExporterPrice

sort_resource_number_aluminium = {
	clear_array = aluminiumCurrentImport
	every_other_country = { 
		for_loop_effect = {
			end = aluminiumExportTarget^num
			value = v
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { aluminiumExportTarget^v = ROOT.id }
				}
				add_to_array = { ROOT.aluminiumCurrentImport = aluminiumExportTargetAmount^i }

			}
			else = {
				add_to_array = { ROOT.aluminiumCurrentImport = 0 }
			}
		}
	}
	#Creates the Sample Array	
	resize_temp_array = {
		array = aluminiumExporterInfo_temp #values
		size = aluminiumExporterInfo^num
	}
	resize_temp_array = {
		array = aluminiumExporters_temp #tags
		size = aluminiumExporterInfo^num
	}

	for_loop_effect = {
		end = aluminiumExporterInfo^num
		value = v
		find_highest_in_array = {
			array = aluminiumExporterInfo
			value = max
			index = max_index
		}
		set_temp_variable = { aluminiumExporterInfo_temp^v = max } #sets biggest at the earliest available position
		set_temp_variable = { aluminiumExporters_temp^v = aluminiumExporters^max_index } #sets biggest TAG at the earliest available position
		set_variable = { aluminiumExporterInfo^max_index = -1 } #removes it from selection
	}
	clear_array = aluminiumExporterInfo
	clear_array = aluminiumExporters
	#restores destroyed arrays in the above process - i.e. if they were deleted due to being less then 1%
	for_each_loop = {
		array = aluminiumExporterInfo_temp
		add_to_array = { aluminiumExporterInfo = v }
		
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = aluminiumExporters_temp
		add_to_array = { aluminiumExporters = v  }
	}
	clear_temp_array = aluminiumExporterInfo_temp
	clear_temp_array = aluminiumExporters_temp



}



aluminium_import_ai = {
	set_variable = {
		aluminium_total = resource@aluminium
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				aluminium_total < 0
			}
			check_variable = {
				trade_infrastructure_total_available > 0
			}
		}
		generate_available_aluminium_import_array = yes
        sort_price_number_aluminium = yes

        #Loops thru cheapest countries to buy from
        for_each_scope_loop = {
            array = ROOT.aluminiumExporters
            #index = i
            break = cheapest_brk

            #Checks if the country's goods are cheaper & shipping is affordabole 
            #TODO - Shipping cost
            if = {
                limit = {
                    check_variable = { currentAvailablealuminiumExport > 0 }
                    OR = {
                        NOT = { check_variable = { ROOT.trade_infrastructure_total_available < 1 } }
                        NOT = { check_variable = { ROOT.aluminium_total > -1 } }
                    }
                }
                set_variable = {
                    aluminium_need = ROOT.aluminium_total
                }
				multiply_variable = {
					aluminium_need = -1
				}
                if = {
                    limit = {
                        check_variable = {
                            currentAvailablealuminiumExport > aluminium_need
                        }
                    }

                    set_variable = {
                        ROOT.purchase_amount = aluminium_need
                    }
                    add_to_variable = {
                        ROOT.aluminium_total = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }

                    subtract_from_variable = {
                        ROOT.purchase_amount = ROOT.purchase_amount_prior
                    }
                    subtract_from_variable = {
                        currentAvailablealuminiumExport = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }
                    add_to_variable = {
                        ROOT.trade_infrastructure_total_used = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }
                    subtract_from_variable = {
                        ROOT.trade_infrastructure_total_available = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }
                    add_to_variable = {
                        currentExportedaluminium = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }
                    add_to_variable = {
                        ROOT.aluminiumImports = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        aluminiumExportTarget^ROOT = ROOT.id
                    } 
                    add_to_variable = {
                        aluminiumExportTargetAmount^ROOT = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        price_of_goods = aluminiumExporterPrice^ROOT
                    }
                    multiply_variable = {
                        price_of_goods = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }
                    add_to_variable = {
                        aluminiumExportTargetMonthly^ROOT = price_of_goods
                    }
                    add_to_variable = {
                        ROOT.aluminiumImportTargetMonthly^THIS = price_of_goods
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        ROOT.aluminiumImportTarget^THIS = THIS.id
                    }
                    add_to_variable = {
                        ROOT.aluminiumImportTargetAmount^THIS = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        price_of_goods = 0
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        ROOT.purchase_amount = 0
                    }
					for_loop_effect = {
						end = ROOT.Aluminium_Import_List
						value = v
						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { Aluminium_Import_List^v = THIS.id }
							}
							set_country_flag = found
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = found } }
						add_to_array = { ROOT.Aluminium_Import_List = THIS.id }
					}
					clr_country_flag = found
					for_loop_effect = {
						end = Aluminium_Export_List
						value = v
						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { Aluminium_Export_List^v = ROOT.id }
							}
							set_country_flag = found
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = found } }
						add_to_array = { Aluminium_Export_List = ROOT.id }
					}
					clr_country_flag = found
					if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = {
								currentAvailablealuminiumExport < 0
							}
						}
						country_event = trade.5
					}
                }
                else = {
                    set_variable = {
                        aluminium_need = currentAvailablealuminiumExport
                    }			
                    set_variable = {
                        ROOT.purchase_amount = aluminium_need
                    }
                    add_to_variable = {
                        ROOT.aluminium_total = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }
                    subtract_from_variable = {
                        ROOT.purchase_amount = ROOT.purchase_amount_prior
                    }
                    subtract_from_variable = {
                        currentAvailablealuminiumExport = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }
                    add_to_variable = {
                        ROOT.trade_infrastructure_total_used = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }
                    subtract_from_variable = {
                        ROOT.trade_infrastructure_total_available = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }
                    add_to_variable = {
                        currentExportedaluminium = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }
                    add_to_variable = {
                        ROOT.aluminiumImports = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        aluminiumExportTarget^ROOT = ROOT.id
                    } 
                    add_to_variable = {
                        aluminiumExportTargetAmount^ROOT = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        price_of_goods = aluminiumExporterPrice^ROOT
                    }
                    multiply_variable = {
                        price_of_goods = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }
                    add_to_variable = {
                        aluminiumExportTargetMonthly^ROOT = price_of_goods
                    }
                    add_to_variable = {
                        ROOT.aluminiumImportTargetMonthly^THIS = price_of_goods
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        ROOT.aluminiumImportTarget^THIS = THIS.id
                    }
                    add_to_variable = {
                        ROOT.aluminiumImportTargetAmount^THIS = ROOT.purchase_amount
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        price_of_goods = 0
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        ROOT.purchase_amount = 0
                    }
					for_loop_effect = {
						end = ROOT.Aluminium_Import_List
						value = v
						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { Aluminium_Import_List^v = THIS.id }
							}
							set_country_flag = found
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = found } }
						add_to_array = { ROOT.Aluminium_Import_List = THIS.id }
					}
					clr_country_flag = found
					for_loop_effect = {
						end = Aluminium_Export_List
						value = v
						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = { Aluminium_Export_List^v = ROOT.id }
							}
							set_country_flag = found
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = found } }
						add_to_array = { Aluminium_Export_List = ROOT.id }
					}
					clr_country_flag = found
					if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = {
								currentAvailablealuminiumExport < 0
							}
						}
						country_event = trade.5
					}
                }
            }
           if = {
                limit = {
                    OR = {
                        NOT = { check_variable = { ROOT.trade_infrastructure_total_available < 1 } }
                        NOT = { check_variable = { ROOT.aluminium_total > -1 } }
                    }
                }
                set_temp_variable = { cheapest_brk = 1 }
            }
        }
	}
}

	food_import_ai = yes
    oil_import_ai = yes
    steel_import_ai = yes
    aluminium_import_ai = yes
    rareEarth_import_ai = yes
    chromium_import_ai = yes

	clear_array = CgVehiclesT4ImportTargetAmount
	clear_array = CgVehiclesT4ImportTarget
	clear_array = CgVehiclesT4ImportTargetMonthly
	clear_array = CgVehiclesT4ExportTargetMonthly
	clear_array = CgVehiclesT4ExportTargetAmount
	clear_array = CgVehiclesT4ExportTarget


	#Vehicles T5
	add_to_variable = {
		trade_infrastructure_total_available = cgVehiclesT5Imports
	}
	subtract_from_variable = {
		trade_infrastructure_total_used = cgVehiclesT5Imports
	}
	set_variable = { currentAvailableCgVehiclesT5Export = cg_vehicles_export_stock_t5 }
	set_variable = { cgVehiclesT5Imports = 0 }
	set_variable = { currentExportedCgVehiclesT5 = 0 }
	clear_array = CgVehiclesT5ImportTargetAmount
	clear_array = CgVehiclesT5ImportTarget
	clear_array = CgVehiclesT5ImportTargetMonthly
	clear_array = CgVehiclesT5ExportTargetMonthly
	clear_array = CgVehiclesT5ExportTargetAmount
	clear_array = CgVehiclesT5ExportTarget


	#Consumer Electronics T1
	add_to_variable = {
		trade_infrastructure_total_available = cgElectronicsT1Imports
	}
	subtract_from_variable = {
		trade_infrastructure_total_used = cgElectronicsT1Imports
	}

	set_variable = { cgElectronicsT1Imports = 0 }
	set_variable = { currentExportedCgElectronicsT1 = 0 }

#consumer goods section
# Basic Consumer Goods Import Listing Effects

generate_available_cg_basic_t0_import_array = {
	clear_array = ROOT.cgBasicT0Exporters
	clear_array = ROOT.cgBasicT0ExporterInfo

	every_other_country = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { cg_basic_export_stock_t0 > 0.99 }
				check_variable = { currentExportedCgBasicT0 > 0.99 }
			}
			NOT = { tag = ROOT }
			check_variable = { ROOT.trade_embargo_check^THIS = 0 }
			NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
		}

		add_to_array = { ROOT.cgBasicT0Exporters = THIS.id }
		add_to_array = { ROOT.cgBasicT0ExporterInfo = THIS.cg_basic_export_stock_t0 }

		set_variable = { cgBasicT0ExportCash = global.cgBasicT0BasePrice }

		set_variable = { price_Adj = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { price_Adjuster = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { price_Adjuster = ROOT.price_adjust_cg_basic_t0^THIS }

		if = {
			limit = { is_subject_of = ROOT }
			set_variable = { price_Adj = modifier@extra_trade_to_overlord_factor }
			set_variable = { price_Adj2 = 1 }
			subtract_from_variable = { price_Adj2 = price_Adj }
			set_temp_variable = { price_Adjuster = 1 }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { price_Adjuster = 1 } }
			multiply_variable = { cgBasicT0ExportCash = price_Adj }
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					check_variable = { ROOT.price_adjust_generic^THIS = 0 }
				}
			}
			set_variable = { price_Adjuster = 0 }
			set_variable = { price_Adjuster_Special = ROOT.price_adjust_generic^THIS }
			set_variable = { price_Adj = price_Adjuster_Special }
			set_variable = { price_Adj2 = 1 }
			subtract_from_variable = { price_Adj2 = price_Adj }
			set_temp_variable = { price_Adjuster = 1 }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { price_Adjuster = 1 } }
			multiply_variable = { cgBasicT0ExportCash = price_Adj }
		}

		set_variable = { price_adjustment_ideas = modifier@global_trade_penalty_adjustment }

		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = arab_league_member }
			add_to_variable = {
				price_adjustment_ideas = ROOT.modifier@arab_league_trade_penalty_adjustment
			}
		}

		add_to_variable = { price_adjustment_ideas = 1 }
		multiply_variable = { cgBasicT0ExportCash = price_adjustment_ideas }

		set_variable = { price_adjustment_ideas2 = 1 }
		subtract_from_variable = {
			price_adjustment_ideas2 = modifier@global_trade_bonus_adjustment
		}
		multiply_variable = { cgBasicT0ExportCash = price_adjustment_ideas2 }

		# Shipping Cost
		set_temp_variable = { exporter_capital = capital }
		var:exporter_capital = {
			set_variable = { PREV.shipping_cost = distance_to@ROOT.capital }
		}

		divide_variable = { shipping_cost = global.base_shipping_cost_divisor }
		multiply_variable = { shipping_cost = 100 }
		round_variable = shipping_cost
		divide_variable = { shipping_cost = 100 }

		set_temp_variable = { modifier_temp = shipping_cost_effect }
		add_to_temp_variable = { modifier_temp = 1 }
		multiply_variable = { shipping_cost = modifier_temp }
		add_to_variable = { shipping_cost = 1 }

		set_temp_variable = { true_price = cgBasicT0ExportCash }
		multiply_temp_variable = { true_price = shipping_cost }

		add_to_variable = { cgBasicT0ExportCash = true_price }

		clear_variable = price_Adjuster
		set_variable = { cgBasicT0ExporterPrice^ROOT = cgBasicT0ExportCash }
	}
}

			set_variable = { price_Adj2 = 1 }

		# Shipping Cost

		}

		divide_variable = { shipping_cost = global.base_shipping_cost_divisor }
		multiply_variable = { shipping_cost = 100 }
		round_variable = shipping_cost
		divide_variable = { shipping_cost = 100 }

				check_variable = { currentExportedCgDurableT5 > 0.99 }
			}
			NOT = { tag = ROOT }
			check_variable = { ROOT.trade_embargo_check^THIS = 0 }
			NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
		}

		add_to_array = { ROOT.cgDurableT5Exporters = THIS.id }
		add_to_array = { ROOT.cgDurableT5ExporterInfo = THIS.cg_durable_export_stock_t5 }

		set_variable = { cgDurableT5ExportCash = global.cgDurableT5BasePrice }

		set_variable = { price_Adj = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { price_Adjuster = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { price_Adjuster = ROOT.price_adjust_cg_durable_t5^THIS }

		if = {
			limit = { is_subject_of = ROOT }
			set_variable = { price_Adj = modifier@extra_trade_to_overlord_factor }
			set_variable = { price_Adj2 = 1 }
			subtract_from_variable = { price_Adj2 = price_Adj }
			set_temp_variable = { price_Adjuster = 1 }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { price_Adjuster = 1 } }
			multiply_variable = { cgDurableT5ExportCash = price_Adj }
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					check_variable = { ROOT.price_adjust_generic^THIS = 0 }
				}
			}
			set_variable = { price_Adjuster = 0 }
			set_variable = { price_Adjuster_Special = ROOT.price_adjust_generic^THIS }
			set_variable = { price_Adj = price_Adjuster_Special }
			set_variable = { price_Adj2 = 1 }
			subtract_from_variable = { price_Adj2 = price_Adj }
			set_temp_variable = { price_Adjuster = 1 }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { price_Adjuster = 1 } }
			multiply_variable = { cgDurableT5ExportCash = price_Adj }
		}

		set_variable = { price_adjustment_ideas = modifier@global_trade_penalty_adjustment }

		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = arab_league_member }
			add_to_variable = {
				price_adjustment_ideas = ROOT.modifier@arab_league_trade_penalty_adjustment
			}
		}

		add_to_variable = { price_adjustment_ideas = 1 }
		multiply_variable = { cgDurableT5ExportCash = price_adjustment_ideas }

		set_variable = { price_adjustment_ideas2 = 1 }
		subtract_from_variable = {
			price_adjustment_ideas2 = modifier@global_trade_bonus_adjustment
		}
		multiply_variable = { cgDurableT5ExportCash = price_adjustment_ideas2 }

		# Shipping Cost
		set_temp_variable = { exporter_capital = capital }
		var:exporter_capital = {
			set_variable = { PREV.shipping_cost = distance_to@ROOT.capital }
		}

		divide_variable = { shipping_cost = global.base_shipping_cost_divisor }
		multiply_variable = { shipping_cost = 100 }
		round_variable = shipping_cost
		divide_variable = { shipping_cost = 100 }

		set_temp_variable = { modifier_temp = shipping_cost_effect }
		add_to_temp_variable = { modifier_temp = 1 }
		multiply_variable = { shipping_cost = modifier_temp }
		add_to_variable = { shipping_cost = 1 }

		set_temp_variable = { true_price = cgDurableT5ExportCash }
		multiply_temp_variable = { true_price = shipping_cost }

		add_to_variable = { cgDurableT5ExportCash = true_price }

		clear_variable = price_Adjuster
		set_variable = { cgDurableT5ExporterPrice^ROOT = cgDurableT5ExportCash }
	}
}



# Vehicles Import Listing Effects

generate_available_cg_vehicles_t0_import_array = {
	clear_array = ROOT.cgVehiclesT0Exporters
	clear_array = ROOT.cgVehiclesT0ExporterInfo

	every_other_country = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { cg_vehicles_export_stock_t0 > 0.99 }
				check_variable = { currentExportedCgVehiclesT0 > 0.99 }
			}
			NOT = { tag = ROOT }
			check_variable = { ROOT.trade_embargo_check^THIS = 0 }
			NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
		}

		add_to_array = { ROOT.cgVehiclesT0Exporters = THIS.id }
		add_to_array = { ROOT.cgVehiclesT0ExporterInfo = THIS.cg_vehicles_export_stock_t0 }

		set_variable = { cgVehiclesT0ExportCash = global.cgVehiclesT0BasePrice }

		set_variable = { price_Adj = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { price_Adjuster = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { price_Adjuster = ROOT.price_adjust_cg_vehicles_t0^THIS }

		if = {
			limit = { is_subject_of = ROOT }
			set_variable = { price_Adj = modifier@extra_trade_to_overlord_factor }
			set_variable = { price_Adj2 = 1 }
			subtract_from_variable = { price_Adj2 = price_Adj }
			set_temp_variable = { price_Adjuster = 1 }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { price_Adjuster = 1 } }
			multiply_variable = { cgVehiclesT0ExportCash = price_Adj }
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					check_variable = { ROOT.price_adjust_generic^THIS = 0 }
				}
			}
			set_variable = { price_Adjuster = 0 }
			set_variable = { price_Adjuster_Special = ROOT.price_adjust_generic^THIS }
			set_variable = { price_Adj = price_Adjuster_Special }
			set_variable = { price_Adj2 = 1 }
			subtract_from_variable = { price_Adj2 = price_Adj }
			set_temp_variable = { price_Adjuster = 1 }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { price_Adjuster = 1 } }
			multiply_variable = { cgVehiclesT0ExportCash = price_Adj }
		}

		set_variable = { price_adjustment_ideas = modifier@global_trade_penalty_adjustment }

		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = arab_league_member }
			add_to_variable = {
				price_adjustment_ideas = ROOT.modifier@arab_league_trade_penalty_adjustment
			}
		}

		add_to_variable = { price_adjustment_ideas = 1 }
		multiply_variable = { cgVehiclesT0ExportCash = price_adjustment_ideas }

		set_variable = { price_adjustment_ideas2 = 1 }
		subtract_from_variable = {
			price_adjustment_ideas2 = modifier@global_trade_bonus_adjustment
		}
		multiply_variable = { cgVehiclesT0ExportCash = price_adjustment_ideas2 }

		# Shipping Cost
		set_temp_variable = { exporter_capital = capital }
		var:exporter_capital = {
			set_variable = { PREV.shipping_cost = distance_to@ROOT.capital }
		}

		divide_variable = { shipping_cost = global.base_shipping_cost_divisor }
		multiply_variable = { shipping_cost = 100 }
		round_variable = shipping_cost
		divide_variable = { shipping_cost = 100 }

		set_temp_variable = { modifier_temp = shipping_cost_effect }
		add_to_temp_variable = { modifier_temp = 1 }
		multiply_variable = { shipping_cost = modifier_temp }
		add_to_variable = { shipping_cost = 1 }

		set_temp_variable = { true_price = cgVehiclesT0ExportCash }
		multiply_temp_variable = { true_price = shipping_cost }

		add_to_variable = { cgVehiclesT0ExportCash = true_price }

		clear_variable = price_Adjuster
		set_variable = { cgVehiclesT0ExporterPrice^ROOT = cgVehiclesT0ExportCash }
	}
}

		add_to_array = { ROOT.cgVehiclesT5Exporters = THIS.id }
		add_to_array = { ROOT.cgVehiclesT5ExporterInfo = THIS.cg_vehicles_export_stock_t5 }

		set_variable = { cgVehiclesT5ExportCash = global.cgVehiclesT5BasePrice }

		set_variable = { price_Adj = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { price_Adjuster = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { price_Adjuster = ROOT.price_adjust_cg_vehicles_t5^THIS }

		if = {
			limit = { is_subject_of = ROOT }
			set_variable = { price_Adj = modifier@extra_trade_to_overlord_factor }
			set_variable = { price_Adj2 = 1 }
			subtract_from_variable = { price_Adj2 = price_Adj }
			set_temp_variable = { price_Adjuster = 1 }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { price_Adjuster = 1 } }
			multiply_variable = { cgVehiclesT5ExportCash = price_Adj }
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					check_variable = { ROOT.price_adjust_generic^THIS = 0 }
				}
			}
			set_variable = { price_Adjuster = 0 }
			set_variable = { price_Adjuster_Special = ROOT.price_adjust_generic^THIS }
			set_variable = { price_Adj = price_Adjuster_Special }
			set_variable = { price_Adj2 = 1 }
			subtract_from_variable = { price_Adj2 = price_Adj }
			set_temp_variable = { price_Adjuster = 1 }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { price_Adjuster = 1 } }
			multiply_variable = { cgVehiclesT5ExportCash = price_Adj }
		}

		set_variable = { price_adjustment_ideas = modifier@global_trade_penalty_adjustment }

		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = arab_league_member }
			add_to_variable = {
				price_adjustment_ideas = ROOT.modifier@arab_league_trade_penalty_adjustment
			}
		}

		add_to_variable = { price_adjustment_ideas = 1 }
		multiply_variable = { cgVehiclesT5ExportCash = price_adjustment_ideas }

		set_variable = { price_adjustment_ideas2 = 1 }
		subtract_from_variable = {
			price_adjustment_ideas2 = modifier@global_trade_bonus_adjustment
		}
		multiply_variable = { cgVehiclesT5ExportCash = price_adjustment_ideas2 }

		# Shipping Cost
		set_temp_variable = { exporter_capital = capital }
		var:exporter_capital = {
			set_variable = { PREV.shipping_cost = distance_to@ROOT.capital }
		}

		divide_variable = { shipping_cost = global.base_shipping_cost_divisor }
		multiply_variable = { shipping_cost = 100 }
		round_variable = shipping_cost
		divide_variable = { shipping_cost = 100 }

		set_temp_variable = { modifier_temp = shipping_cost_effect }
		add_to_temp_variable = { modifier_temp = 1 }
		multiply_variable = { shipping_cost = modifier_temp }
		add_to_variable = { shipping_cost = 1 }

		set_temp_variable = { true_price = cgVehiclesT5ExportCash }
		multiply_temp_variable = { true_price = shipping_cost }

		add_to_variable = { cgVehiclesT5ExportCash = true_price }

		clear_variable = price_Adjuster
		set_variable = { cgVehiclesT5ExporterPrice^ROOT = cgVehiclesT5ExportCash }
	}
}



# Consumer Electronics Import Listing Effects

generate_available_cg_consumer_electronics_t1_import_array = {
	clear_array = ROOT.cgElectronicsT1Exporters
	clear_array = ROOT.cgElectronicsT1ExporterInfo

	every_other_country = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { cg_consumer_electronics_export_stock_t1 > 0.99 }
				check_variable = { currentExportedCgElectronicsT1 > 0.99 }
			}
			NOT = { tag = ROOT }
			check_variable = { ROOT.trade_embargo_check^THIS = 0 }
			NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
		}

		add_to_array = { ROOT.cgElectronicsT1Exporters = THIS.id }
		add_to_array = { ROOT.cgElectronicsT1ExporterInfo = THIS.cg_consumer_electronics_export_stock_t1 }

		set_variable = { cgElectronicsT1ExportCash = global.cgElectronicsT1BasePrice }

		set_variable = { price_Adj = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { price_Adjuster = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { price_Adjuster = ROOT.price_adjust_cg_electronics_t1^THIS }

		if = {
			limit = { is_subject_of = ROOT }
			set_variable = { price_Adj = modifier@extra_trade_to_overlord_factor }
			set_variable = { price_Adj2 = 1 }
			subtract_from_variable = { price_Adj2 = price_Adj }
			set_temp_variable = { price_Adjuster = 1 }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { price_Adjuster = 1 } }
			multiply_variable = { cgElectronicsT1ExportCash = price_Adj }
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					check_variable = { ROOT.price_adjust_generic^THIS = 0 }
				}
			}
			set_variable = { price_Adjuster = 0 }
			set_variable = { price_Adjuster_Special = ROOT.price_adjust_generic^THIS }
			set_variable = { price_Adj = price_Adjuster_Special }
			set_variable = { price_Adj2 = 1 }
			subtract_from_variable = { price_Adj2 = price_Adj }
			set_temp_variable = { price_Adjuster = 1 }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { price_Adjuster = 1 } }
			multiply_variable = { cgElectronicsT1ExportCash = price_Adj }
		}

		set_variable = { price_adjustment_ideas = modifier@global_trade_penalty_adjustment }

		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = arab_league_member }
			add_to_variable = {
				price_adjustment_ideas = ROOT.modifier@arab_league_trade_penalty_adjustment
			}
		}

		add_to_variable = { price_adjustment_ideas = 1 }
		multiply_variable = { cgElectronicsT1ExportCash = price_adjustment_ideas }

		set_variable = { price_adjustment_ideas2 = 1 }
		subtract_from_variable = {
			price_adjustment_ideas2 = modifier@global_trade_bonus_adjustment
		}
		multiply_variable = { cgElectronicsT1ExportCash = price_adjustment_ideas2 }

		# Shipping Cost
		set_temp_variable = { exporter_capital = capital }
		var:exporter_capital = {
			set_variable = { PREV.shipping_cost = distance_to@ROOT.capital }
		}

		divide_variable = { shipping_cost = global.base_shipping_cost_divisor }
		multiply_variable = { shipping_cost = 100 }
		round_variable = shipping_cost
		divide_variable = { shipping_cost = 100 }

		set_temp_variable = { modifier_temp = shipping_cost_effect }
		add_to_temp_variable = { modifier_temp = 1 }
		multiply_variable = { shipping_cost = modifier_temp }
		add_to_variable = { shipping_cost = 1 }

		set_temp_variable = { true_price = cgElectronicsT1ExportCash }
		multiply_temp_variable = { true_price = shipping_cost }

		add_to_variable = { cgElectronicsT1ExportCash = true_price }

		clear_variable = price_Adjuster
		set_variable = { cgElectronicsT1ExporterPrice^ROOT = cgElectronicsT1ExportCash }
	}
}

# Consumer Goods Buy Effects

buy_cg_basic_t0 = {

	var:currentPurchaser = {
		for_loop_effect = {
			end = ROOT.CgBasicT0_Import_List
			value = v
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { CgBasicT0_Import_List^v = THIS.id }
				}
				set_country_flag = found
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = found } }
			add_to_array = { ROOT.CgBasicT0_Import_List = THIS.id }
		}
		clr_country_flag = found

		for_loop_effect = {
			end = CgBasicT0_Export_List
			value = v
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { CgBasicT0_Export_List^v = ROOT.id }
				}
				set_country_flag = found
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = found } }
			add_to_array = { CgBasicT0_Export_List = ROOT.id }
		}
		clr_country_flag = found

		subtract_from_variable = {
			ROOT.purchase_amount = ROOT.purchase_amount_prior
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			currentAvailableCgBasicT0Export = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			ROOT.trade_infrastructure_total_used = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			ROOT.trade_infrastructure_total_available = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			currentExportedCgBasicT0 = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			ROOT.cgBasicT0Imports = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}

		set_variable = {
			CgBasicT0ExportTarget^ROOT = ROOT.id
		} 
		add_to_variable = {
			CgBasicT0ExportTargetAmount^ROOT = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}

		set_variable = {
			price_of_goods = cgBasicT0ExporterPrice^ROOT
		}
		multiply_variable = {
			price_of_goods = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}

		add_to_variable = {
			CgBasicT0ExportTargetMonthly^ROOT = price_of_goods
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			ROOT.CgBasicT0ImportTargetMonthly^THIS = price_of_goods
		}
		set_variable = {
			ROOT.CgBasicT0ImportTarget^THIS = THIS.id
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			ROOT.CgBasicT0ImportTargetAmount^THIS = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}

		set_variable = {
			price_of_goods = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			ROOT.purchase_amount = 0
		}
	}

	every_other_country = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				cg_basic_export_stock_t0 > 0.99
			}
			NOT = {
				tag = ROOT
			}
			check_variable = {
				ROOT.trade_embargo_check^THIS = 0
			}
			NOT = {
				has_war_with = ROOT
			}
		}
		clear_array = CgBasicT0CurrentExport
		for_loop_effect = {
			end = CgBasicT0ExportTarget^num
			value = v
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { CgBasicT0ExportTarget^v = ROOT.id }
				}
				add_to_array = { CgBasicT0CurrentExport = CgBasicT0ExportTargetAmount^i }

			}
			else = {
				add_to_array = { CgBasicT0CurrentExport = 0 }

			}
		}
	}
}

				ROOT.trade_embargo_check^THIS = 0
			}
			NOT = {
				has_war_with = ROOT
			}
		}
		clear_array = CgDurableT0CurrentExport
		for_loop_effect = {
			end = CgDurableT0ExportTarget^num
			value = v
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { CgDurableT0ExportTarget^v = ROOT.id }
				}
				add_to_array = { CgDurableT0CurrentExport = CgDurableT0ExportTargetAmount^i }

			}
			else = {
				add_to_array = { CgDurableT0CurrentExport = 0 }

			}
		}
	}
}

buy_cg_durable_t1 = {

	var:currentPurchaser = {
		for_loop_effect = {
			end = ROOT.CgDurableT1_Import_List
			value = v
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { CgDurableT1_Import_List^v = THIS.id }
				}
				set_country_flag = found
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = found } }
			add_to_array = { ROOT.CgDurableT1_Import_List = THIS.id }
		}
		clr_country_flag = found

		for_loop_effect = {
			end = CgDurableT1_Export_List
			value = v
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { CgDurableT1_Export_List^v = ROOT.id }
				}
				set_country_flag = found
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = found } }
			add_to_array = { CgDurableT1_Export_List = ROOT.id }
		}
		clr_country_flag = found

		subtract_from_variable = {
			ROOT.purchase_amount = ROOT.purchase_amount_prior
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			currentAvailableCgDurableT1Export = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			ROOT.trade_infrastructure_total_used = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			ROOT.trade_infrastructure_total_available = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			currentExportedCgDurableT1 = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			ROOT.cgDurableT1Imports = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}

		set_variable = {
			CgDurableT1ExportTarget^ROOT = ROOT.id
		} 
		add_to_variable = {
			CgDurableT1ExportTargetAmount^ROOT = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}

		set_variable = {
			price_of_goods = cgDurableT1ExporterPrice^ROOT
		}
		multiply_variable = {
			price_of_goods = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}

		add_to_variable = {
			CgDurableT1ExportTargetMonthly^ROOT = price_of_goods
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			ROOT.CgDurableT1ImportTargetMonthly^THIS = price_of_goods
		}
		set_variable = {
			ROOT.CgDurableT1ImportTarget^THIS = THIS.id
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			ROOT.CgDurableT1ImportTargetAmount^THIS = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}

		set_variable = {
			price_of_goods = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			ROOT.purchase_amount = 0
		}
	}

	every_other_country = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				cg_durable_export_stock_t1 > 0.99
			}
			NOT = {
				tag = ROOT
			}
			check_variable = {
				ROOT.trade_embargo_check^THIS = 0
			}
			NOT = {
				has_war_with = ROOT
			}
		}
		clear_array = CgDurableT1CurrentExport
		for_loop_effect = {
			end = CgDurableT1ExportTarget^num
			value = v
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { CgDurableT1ExportTarget^v = ROOT.id }
				}
				add_to_array = { CgDurableT1CurrentExport = CgDurableT1ExportTargetAmount^i }

			}
			else = {
				add_to_array = { CgDurableT1CurrentExport = 0 }

			}
		}
	}
}

			ROOT.purchase_amount = ROOT.purchase_amount_prior
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			currentAvailableCgVehiclesT1Export = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			ROOT.trade_infrastructure_total_used = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			ROOT.trade_infrastructure_total_available = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			currentExportedCgVehiclesT1 = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			ROOT.cgVehiclesT1Imports = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}

		set_variable = {
			CgVehiclesT1ExportTarget^ROOT = ROOT.id
		} 
		add_to_variable = {
			CgVehiclesT1ExportTargetAmount^ROOT = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}

		set_variable = {
			price_of_goods = cgVehiclesT1ExporterPrice^ROOT
		}
		multiply_variable = {
			price_of_goods = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}

		add_to_variable = {
			CgVehiclesT1ExportTargetMonthly^ROOT = price_of_goods
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			ROOT.CgVehiclesT1ImportTargetMonthly^THIS = price_of_goods
		}
		set_variable = {
			ROOT.CgVehiclesT1ImportTarget^THIS = THIS.id
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			ROOT.CgVehiclesT1ImportTargetAmount^THIS = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}

		set_variable = {
			price_of_goods = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			ROOT.purchase_amount = 0
		}
	}

	every_other_country = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				cg_vehicles_export_stock_t1 > 0.99
			}
			NOT = {
				tag = ROOT
			}
			check_variable = {
				ROOT.trade_embargo_check^THIS = 0
			}
			NOT = {
				has_war_with = ROOT
			}
		}
		clear_array = CgVehiclesT1CurrentExport
		for_loop_effect = {
			end = CgVehiclesT1ExportTarget^num
			value = v
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { CgVehiclesT1ExportTarget^v = ROOT.id }
				}
				add_to_array = { CgVehiclesT1CurrentExport = CgVehiclesT1ExportTargetAmount^i }

			}
			else = {
				add_to_array = { CgVehiclesT1CurrentExport = 0 }

			}
		}
	}
}

			ROOT.CgElectronicsT2ImportTargetAmount^THIS = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}

		set_variable = {
			price_of_goods = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			ROOT.purchase_amount = 0
		}
	}

	every_other_country = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				cg_consumer_electronics_export_stock_t2 > 0.99
			}
			NOT = {
				tag = ROOT
			}
			check_variable = {
				ROOT.trade_embargo_check^THIS = 0
			}
			NOT = {
				has_war_with = ROOT
			}
		}
		clear_array = CgElectronicsT2CurrentExport
		for_loop_effect = {
			end = CgElectronicsT2ExportTarget^num
			value = v
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { CgElectronicsT2ExportTarget^v = ROOT.id }
				}
				add_to_array = { CgElectronicsT2CurrentExport = CgElectronicsT2ExportTargetAmount^i }

			}
			else = {
				add_to_array = { CgElectronicsT2CurrentExport = 0 }

			}
		}
	}
}

buy_cg_electronics_t3 = {

	var:currentPurchaser = {
		for_loop_effect = {
			end = ROOT.CgElectronicsT3_Import_List
			value = v
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { CgElectronicsT3_Import_List^v = THIS.id }
				}
				set_country_flag = found
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = found } }
			add_to_array = { ROOT.CgElectronicsT3_Import_List = THIS.id }
		}
		clr_country_flag = found

		for_loop_effect = {
			end = CgElectronicsT3_Export_List
			value = v
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { CgElectronicsT3_Export_List^v = ROOT.id }
				}
				set_country_flag = found
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = found } }
			add_to_array = { CgElectronicsT3_Export_List = ROOT.id }
		}
		clr_country_flag = found

		subtract_from_variable = {
			ROOT.purchase_amount = ROOT.purchase_amount_prior
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			currentAvailableCgElectronicsT3Export = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			ROOT.trade_infrastructure_total_used = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		subtract_from_variable = {
			ROOT.trade_infrastructure_total_available = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			currentExportedCgElectronicsT3 = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			ROOT.cgElectronicsT3Imports = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}

		set_variable = {
			CgElectronicsT3ExportTarget^ROOT = ROOT.id
		} 
		add_to_variable = {
			CgElectronicsT3ExportTargetAmount^ROOT = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}

		set_variable = {
			price_of_goods = cgElectronicsT3ExporterPrice^ROOT
		}
		multiply_variable = {
			price_of_goods = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}

		add_to_variable = {
			CgElectronicsT3ExportTargetMonthly^ROOT = price_of_goods
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			ROOT.CgElectronicsT3ImportTargetMonthly^THIS = price_of_goods
		}
		set_variable = {
			ROOT.CgElectronicsT3ImportTarget^THIS = THIS.id
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			ROOT.CgElectronicsT3ImportTargetAmount^THIS = ROOT.purchase_amount
		}

		set_variable = {
			price_of_goods = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			ROOT.purchase_amount = 0
		}
	}

	every_other_country = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				cg_consumer_electronics_export_stock_t3 > 0.99
			}
			NOT = {
				tag = ROOT
			}
			check_variable = {
				ROOT.trade_embargo_check^THIS = 0
			}
			NOT = {
				has_war_with = ROOT
			}
		}
		clear_array = CgElectronicsT3CurrentExport
		for_loop_effect = {
			end = CgElectronicsT3ExportTarget^num
			value = v
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { CgElectronicsT3ExportTarget^v = ROOT.id }
				}
				add_to_array = { CgElectronicsT3CurrentExport = CgElectronicsT3ExportTargetAmount^i }

			}
			else = {
				add_to_array = { CgElectronicsT3CurrentExport = 0 }

			}
		}
	}
}

set_all_import_list_variables_to_zero = {
		set_variable = { steel_trade = 0 }
		set_variable = { nuclear_trade = 0 }
		set_variable = { rare_earth_trade = 0 }
		set_variable = { food_trade = 0 }
		set_variable = { aluminum_trade = 0 }
		set_variable = { oil_trade = 0 }
		set_variable = { nuclear_trade = 0 }
		set_variable = { basic_trade = 0 }
		set_variable = { durable_trade = 0 }
		set_variable = { automative_trade = 0 }
		set_variable = { electronics_trade = 0 }
}
# Consumer Goods Sort Price Effects

sort_price_number_cg_basic_t0 = {
	clear_array = cgBasicT0ExportPriceRate

	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = cgBasicT0Exporters
		add_to_array = { 
			PREV.cgBasicT0ExportPriceRate = cgBasicT0ExporterPrice^ROOT
		}
	}

	clear_array = cgBasicT0CurrentImport

	every_other_country = { 
		for_loop_effect = {
			end = CgBasicT0ExportTarget^num
			value = v
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { CgBasicT0ExportTarget^v = ROOT.id }
				}
				add_to_array = { ROOT.cgBasicT0CurrentImport = CgBasicT0ExportTargetAmount^i }
			}
			else = {
				add_to_array = { ROOT.cgBasicT0CurrentImport = 0 }
			}
		}
	}

	# Creates the Sample Array
	resize_temp_array = {
		array = cgBasicT0ExporterInfo_temp
		size = cgBasicT0ExporterInfo^num
	}
	resize_temp_array = {
		array = cgBasicT0ExportPriceRate_temp
		size = cgBasicT0ExporterInfo^num
	}
	resize_temp_array = {
		array = cgBasicT0Exporters_temp
		size = cgBasicT0ExporterInfo^num
	}

	set_temp_variable = { start_temp = cgBasicT0ExportPriceRate^num }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { start_temp = 1 }

	for_loop_effect = {
		start = start_temp
		end = 0
		compare = greater_than_or_equals
		add = -1
		value = v

		find_highest_in_array = {
			array = cgBasicT0ExportPriceRate
			value = max
			index = max_index
		}

		set_temp_variable = { cgBasicT0ExportPriceRate_temp^v = max }
		set_temp_variable = { cgBasicT0ExporterInfo_temp^v = cgBasicT0Exporters^max_index }
		set_temp_variable = { cgBasicT0Exporters_temp^v = cgBasicT0Exporters^max_index }
		set_variable = { cgBasicT0ExportPriceRate^max_index = -1 }
	}

	clear_array = cgBasicT0ExporterInfo
	clear_array = cgBasicT0Exporters
	clear_array = cgBasicT0ExportPriceRate

	# Restores destroyed arrays in the above process
	for_each_loop = {
		array = cgBasicT0ExportPriceRate_temp
		add_to_array = { cgBasicT0ExportPriceRate = v }
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = cgBasicT0ExporterInfo_temp
		add_to_array = { cgBasicT0ExporterInfo = v }
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = cgBasicT0Exporters_temp
		add_to_array = { cgBasicT0Exporters = v }
	}

	clear_temp_array = cgBasicT0ExporterInfo_temp
	clear_temp_array = cgBasicT0Exporters_temp
	clear_temp_array = cgBasicT0ExportPriceRate_temp
}

	clear_temp_array = cgBasicT2Exporters_temp
	clear_temp_array = cgBasicT2ExportPriceRate_temp

sort_price_number_cg_basic_t3 = {
	clear_array = cgBasicT3ExportPriceRate

	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = cgBasicT3Exporters
		add_to_array = { 
			PREV.cgBasicT3ExportPriceRate = cgBasicT3ExporterPrice^ROOT
		}
	}

	clear_array = cgBasicT3CurrentImport

	every_other_country = { 
		for_loop_effect = {
			end = CgBasicT3ExportTarget^num
			value = v
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { CgBasicT3ExportTarget^v = ROOT.id }
				}
				add_to_array = { ROOT.cgBasicT3CurrentImport = CgBasicT3ExportTargetAmount^i }
			}
			else = {
				add_to_array = { ROOT.cgBasicT3CurrentImport = 0 }
			}
		}
	}

	# Creates the Sample Array
	resize_temp_array = {
		array = cgBasicT3ExporterInfo_temp
		size = cgBasicT3ExporterInfo^num
	}
	resize_temp_array = {
		array = cgBasicT3ExportPriceRate_temp
		size = cgBasicT3ExporterInfo^num
	}
	resize_temp_array = {
		array = cgBasicT3Exporters_temp
		size = cgBasicT3ExporterInfo^num
	}

	set_temp_variable = { start_temp = cgBasicT3ExportPriceRate^num }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { start_temp = 1 }

	for_loop_effect = {
		start = start_temp
		end = 0
		compare = greater_than_or_equals
		add = -1
		value = v

		find_highest_in_array = {
			array = cgBasicT3ExportPriceRate
			value = max
			index = max_index
		}

		set_temp_variable = { cgBasicT3ExportPriceRate_temp^v = max }
		set_temp_variable = { cgBasicT3ExporterInfo_temp^v = cgBasicT3Exporters^max_index }
		set_temp_variable = { cgBasicT3Exporters_temp^v = cgBasicT3Exporters^max_index }
		set_variable = { cgBasicT3ExportPriceRate^max_index = -1 }
	}

	clear_array = cgBasicT3ExporterInfo
	clear_array = cgBasicT3Exporters
	clear_array = cgBasicT3ExportPriceRate

	# Restores destroyed arrays in the above process
	for_each_loop = {
		array = cgBasicT3ExportPriceRate_temp
		add_to_array = { cgBasicT3ExportPriceRate = v }
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = cgBasicT3ExporterInfo_temp
		add_to_array = { cgBasicT3ExporterInfo = v }
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = cgBasicT3Exporters_temp
		add_to_array = { cgBasicT3Exporters = v }
	}

	clear_temp_array = cgBasicT3ExporterInfo_temp
	clear_temp_array = cgBasicT3Exporters_temp
	clear_temp_array = cgBasicT3ExportPriceRate_temp
}

					check_variable = { CgDurableT5ExportTarget^v = ROOT.id }
				}
				add_to_array = { ROOT.cgDurableT5CurrentImport = CgDurableT5ExportTargetAmount^i }
			}
			else = {
				add_to_array = { ROOT.cgDurableT5CurrentImport = 0 }
			}
		}
	}

	# Creates the Sample Array
	resize_temp_array = {
		array = cgDurableT5ExporterInfo_temp
		size = cgDurableT5ExporterInfo^num
	}
	resize_temp_array = {
		array = cgDurableT5ExportPriceRate_temp
		size = cgDurableT5ExporterInfo^num
	}
	resize_temp_array = {
		array = cgDurableT5Exporters_temp
		size = cgDurableT5ExporterInfo^num
	}

	set_temp_variable = { start_temp = cgDurableT5ExportPriceRate^num }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { start_temp = 1 }

	for_loop_effect = {
		start = start_temp
		end = 0
		compare = greater_than_or_equals
		add = -1
		value = v

		find_highest_in_array = {
			array = cgDurableT5ExportPriceRate
			value = max
			index = max_index
		}

		set_temp_variable = { cgDurableT5ExportPriceRate_temp^v = max }
		set_temp_variable = { cgDurableT5ExporterInfo_temp^v = cgDurableT5Exporters^max_index }
		set_temp_variable = { cgDurableT5Exporters_temp^v = cgDurableT5Exporters^max_index }
		set_variable = { cgDurableT5ExportPriceRate^max_index = -1 }
	}

	clear_array = cgDurableT5ExporterInfo
	clear_array = cgDurableT5Exporters
	clear_array = cgDurableT5ExportPriceRate

	# Restores destroyed arrays in the above process
	for_each_loop = {
		array = cgDurableT5ExportPriceRate_temp
		add_to_array = { cgDurableT5ExportPriceRate = v }
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = cgDurableT5ExporterInfo_temp
		add_to_array = { cgDurableT5ExporterInfo = v }
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = cgDurableT5Exporters_temp
		add_to_array = { cgDurableT5Exporters = v }
	}

	clear_temp_array = cgDurableT5ExporterInfo_temp
	clear_temp_array = cgDurableT5Exporters_temp
	clear_temp_array = cgDurableT5ExportPriceRate_temp

	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = cgElectronicsT2Exporters
		add_to_array = { 
			PREV.cgElectronicsT2ExportPriceRate = cgElectronicsT2ExporterPrice^ROOT
		}
	}

	clear_array = cgElectronicsT2CurrentImport

	every_other_country = { 
		for_loop_effect = {
			end = CgElectronicsT2ExportTarget^num
			value = v
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { CgElectronicsT2ExportTarget^v = ROOT.id }
				}
				add_to_array = { ROOT.cgElectronicsT2CurrentImport = CgElectronicsT2ExportTargetAmount^i }
			}
			else = {
				add_to_array = { ROOT.cgElectronicsT2CurrentImport = 0 }
			}
		}
	}

	# Creates the Sample Array
	resize_temp_array = {
		array = cgElectronicsT2ExporterInfo_temp
		size = cgElectronicsT2ExporterInfo^num
	}
	resize_temp_array = {
		array = cgElectronicsT2ExportPriceRate_temp
		size = cgElectronicsT2ExporterInfo^num
	}
	resize_temp_array = {
		array = cgElectronicsT2Exporters_temp
		size = cgElectronicsT2ExporterInfo^num
	}

	set_temp_variable = { start_temp = cgElectronicsT2ExportPriceRate^num }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { start_temp = 1 }

	for_loop_effect = {
		start = start_temp
		end = 0
		compare = greater_than_or_equals
		add = -1
		value = v

		find_highest_in_array = {
			array = cgElectronicsT2ExportPriceRate
			value = max
			index = max_index
		}

		set_temp_variable = { cgElectronicsT2ExportPriceRate_temp^v = max }
		set_temp_variable = { cgElectronicsT2ExporterInfo_temp^v = cgElectronicsT2Exporters^max_index }
		set_temp_variable = { cgElectronicsT2Exporters_temp^v = cgElectronicsT2Exporters^max_index }
		set_variable = { cgElectronicsT2ExportPriceRate^max_index = -1 }
	}

	clear_array = cgElectronicsT2ExporterInfo
	clear_array = cgElectronicsT2Exporters
	clear_array = cgElectronicsT2ExportPriceRate

	# Restores destroyed arrays in the above process
	for_each_loop = {
		array = cgElectronicsT2ExportPriceRate_temp
		add_to_array = { cgElectronicsT2ExportPriceRate = v }
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = cgElectronicsT2ExporterInfo_temp
		add_to_array = { cgElectronicsT2ExporterInfo = v }
	}
	for_each_loop = {
		array = cgElectronicsT2Exporters_temp
		add_to_array = { cgElectronicsT2Exporters = v }
	}

	clear_temp_array = cgElectronicsT2ExporterInfo_temp
	clear_temp_array = cgElectronicsT2Exporters_temp
	clear_temp_array = cgElectronicsT2ExportPriceRate_temp
}

cwic_calculate_consumer_goods_export_stock_totals = {
	# Basic
	set_variable = { cg_basic_export_stock_total = 0 }
	add_to_variable = { cg_basic_export_stock_total = cg_basic_export_stock_t0 }
	add_to_variable = { cg_basic_export_stock_total = cg_basic_export_stock_t1 }
	add_to_variable = { cg_basic_export_stock_total = cg_basic_export_stock_t2 }
	add_to_variable = { cg_basic_export_stock_total = cg_basic_export_stock_t3 }
	add_to_variable = { cg_basic_export_stock_total = cg_basic_export_stock_t4 }
	add_to_variable = { cg_basic_export_stock_total = cg_basic_export_stock_t5 }

	# Durable
	set_variable = { cg_durable_export_stock_total = 0 }
	add_to_variable = { cg_durable_export_stock_total = cg_durable_export_stock_t0 }
	add_to_variable = { cg_durable_export_stock_total = cg_durable_export_stock_t1 }
	add_to_variable = { cg_durable_export_stock_total = cg_durable_export_stock_t2 }
	add_to_variable = { cg_durable_export_stock_total = cg_durable_export_stock_t3 }
	add_to_variable = { cg_durable_export_stock_total = cg_durable_export_stock_t4 }
	add_to_variable = { cg_durable_export_stock_total = cg_durable_export_stock_t5 }

	# Vehicles
	set_variable = { cg_vehicles_export_stock_total = 0 }
	add_to_variable = { cg_vehicles_export_stock_total = cg_vehicles_export_stock_t0 }
	add_to_variable = { cg_vehicles_export_stock_total = cg_vehicles_export_stock_t1 }
	add_to_variable = { cg_vehicles_export_stock_total = cg_vehicles_export_stock_t2 }
	add_to_variable = { cg_vehicles_export_stock_total = cg_vehicles_export_stock_t3 }
	add_to_variable = { cg_vehicles_export_stock_total = cg_vehicles_export_stock_t4 }
	add_to_variable = { cg_vehicles_export_stock_total = cg_vehicles_export_stock_t5 }

	# Consumer Electronics
	set_variable = { cg_consumer_electronics_export_stock_total = 0 }
	add_to_variable = { cg_consumer_electronics_export_stock_total = cg_consumer_electronics_export_stock_t1 }
	add_to_variable = { cg_consumer_electronics_export_stock_total = cg_consumer_electronics_export_stock_t2 }
	add_to_variable = { cg_consumer_electronics_export_stock_total = cg_consumer_electronics_export_stock_t3 }
	add_to_variable = { cg_consumer_electronics_export_stock_total = cg_consumer_electronics_export_stock_t4 }
	add_to_variable = { cg_consumer_electronics_export_stock_total = cg_consumer_electronics_export_stock_t5 }

	# Grand Total
	set_variable = { cg_export_stock_total = 0 }
	add_to_variable = { cg_export_stock_total = cg_basic_export_stock_total }
	add_to_variable = { cg_export_stock_total = cg_durable_export_stock_total }
	add_to_variable = { cg_export_stock_total = cg_vehicles_export_stock_total }
	add_to_variable = { cg_export_stock_total = cg_consumer_electronics_export_stock_total }
}
